# 旅行与住宿

**trip**
英/美 /trɪp/
n. 旅行；出行
v. 绊倒
[义] A journey to a place and back again, especially for pleasure or a short visit. 往返某地的出行，尤指休闲或短途（business trip 出差，day trip 一日游，school trip 学校郊游）。比 journey 更口语、更短；比 travel 更常指具体一次行程。动词 trip 指绊倒——同形异义。
[例] ① We took a weekend trip to the coast. 我们周末去海边短途旅行了一趟。 ② She tripped on the hotel steps and scraped her knee. 她在酒店台阶上绊了一下，擦破了膝盖。
[搭] business trip 出差, day trip 一日游, round trip 往返, take a trip 去旅行, school trip 学校郊游, trip planner 行程规划
[联] journey 旅程, travel 旅行, tour 观光, vacation 假期, destination 目的地, itinerary 行程, hotel 酒店, flight 航班, tourist 游客, sightseeing 观光
[记] 可数：a trip。trip vs. journey：trip 更短更口语；journey 可强调路程本身。travel 已在人生阶段章详述，此处不重复立条

**journey**
英/美 /ˈdʒɜːni/
n. 旅程；行程
v. 旅行（文）
[义] An act of travelling from one place to another, especially when it takes a long time or has emotional weight. 从一地到另一地的旅行，尤指耗时较长或带情感色彩（long journey 长途旅程， onward journey 下一段行程， life's journey 人生旅程）。比 trip 更正式、可更长；safe journey 常用作道别。
[例] ① The journey from the airport to the hotel took three hours in traffic. 从机场到酒店，路上堵了三个小时。 ② Have a safe journey, and text us when you land. 一路平安，落地给我们发个消息。
[搭] long journey 长途旅程, safe journey 一路平安, onward journey 下一段行程, return journey 返程, journey time 行程时间, start the journey 启程
[联] trip 旅行, travel 旅行, destination 目的地, departure 出发, arrival 到达, flight 航班, train 火车, road 路, adventure 冒险, distance 距离
[记] 可数：a journey。动词 journey 较文，口语多用 travel。Bon voyage 法语道别，义近 safe journey

**tour**
英/美 /tʊə(r)/
n. 观光；巡回
v. 游览；巡回演出
[义] A journey for pleasure in which several places are visited, or a series of professional visits arranged in advance. 为休闲而访问多地的旅行，或预先安排的巡回（guided tour 导览， package tour 跟团游， world tour 世界巡演）。动词 tour 指游览或巡回演出/比赛。
[例] ① We joined a guided tour of the old town. 我们参加了老城的导览团。 ② The band is touring Europe this summer. 乐队今年夏天在欧洲巡演。
[搭] guided tour 导览, package tour 跟团游, city tour 城市观光, tour guide 导游, tour operator 旅行社, go on tour 开始巡回
[联] tourist 游客, tourism 旅游业, sightseeing 观光, trip 旅行, excursion 短途游览, attraction 景点, guide 导游, itinerary 行程, landmark 地标
[记] 可数：a tour。tour vs. trip：tour 强调按路线参观多点；trip 泛指出行。tourist / tourism 同根

**tourism**
英/美 /ˈtʊərɪzəm/
n. 旅游业；观光
[义] The business of providing services for people on holiday, and the activity of travelling for pleasure. 为度假者提供服务所构成的产业，以及为休闲而旅行的活动（international tourism 国际旅游， tourism industry 旅游业， sustainable tourism 可持续旅游）。不可数。
[例] ① Tourism is the main source of income for the island. 旅游业是该岛的主要收入来源。 ② Overtourism has crowded historic city centers. 过度旅游使历史古城中心人满为患。
[搭] international tourism 国际旅游, tourism industry 旅游业, sustainable tourism 可持续旅游, tourism board 旅游局, mass tourism 大众旅游, eco-tourism 生态旅游
[联] tourist 游客, tour 观光, hotel 酒店, destination 目的地, attraction 景点, travel 旅行, resort 度假村, guide 导游, economy 经济, visitor 访客
[记] 不可数。形容词 tourist（游客的）。overtourism 近年常用，指景点人满为患

**tourist**
英/美 /ˈtʊərɪst/
n. 游客；旅游者
[义] A person who is travelling or visiting a place for pleasure. 为休闲而出行或参观某地的人（foreign tourist 外国游客， tourist attraction 旅游景点， tourist visa 旅游签证）。可数。略正式场合可用 visitor；tourist 更绑度假、观光。
[例] ① The museum gets millions of tourists every year. 这家博物馆每年接待数百万游客。 ② Locals complained that tourist buses blocked the narrow streets. 当地居民抱怨旅游大巴堵住了窄街。
[搭] foreign tourist 外国游客, tourist attraction 旅游景点, tourist information 旅游咨询, tourist season 旅游旺季, tourist trap 专坑游客的地方, tourist visa 旅游签证
[联] tourism 旅游业, tour 观光, sightseeing 观光, trip 旅行, guide 导游, hotel 酒店, souvenir 纪念品, destination 目的地, crowd 人群, local 当地人
[记] 可数：a tourist。tourist trap 常带贬义，指价高质低的坑客店。visa 类型之一，护照与签证主条见人生阶段章

**backpacker**
英/美 /ˈbækpækə(r)/
n. 背包客
[义] A person who travels with a backpack, usually on a limited budget and for an extended period. 背着背包旅行的人，通常预算有限、行程较长（backpacker hostel 背包客旅舍， backpacker trail 背包客常走路线）。可数。
[例] ① The hostel is popular with backpackers from all over the world. 这家旅舍很受世界各地背包客欢迎。 ② She spent six months backpacking through Southeast Asia. 她在东南亚背包旅行了六个月。
[搭] backpacker hostel 背包客旅舍, backpacker trail 背包客路线, budget backpacker 预算型背包客, solo backpacker 独自背包客
[联] hostel 旅舍, budget 预算, travel 旅行, hitchhike 搭便车, adventure 冒险, tourist 游客, luggage 行李, map 地图, cheap 便宜的, route 路线
[记] 可数：a backpacker。动词 backpacking 指背包旅行。比 tourist 更强调自助、省钱、长程

**wanderlust**
英/美 /ˈwɒndəlʌst/
n. 旅行癖；漫游冲动
[义] A strong desire to travel and explore the world. 强烈想要旅行、探索世界的冲动（satisfy one's wanderlust 满足旅行欲， wanderlust traveler 爱漫游的旅人）。不可数。社交媒体与旅游文案常见。
[例] ① Her wanderlust led her to quit her job and move abroad. 她的旅行冲动让她辞职移居海外。 ② The photo series captures the wanderlust of a generation. 这组照片捕捉了一代人的漫游渴望。
[源] 德语 *Wanderlust*，*wandern*（漫游）+ *Lust*（欲望）→ 19 世纪借入英语，原指徒步漫游之欲，今泛指旅行冲动。
[搭] satisfy one's wanderlust 满足旅行欲, wanderlust traveler 爱旅行的人, sense of wanderlust 旅行冲动, feed wanderlust 激发旅行欲
[联] travel 旅行, adventure 冒险, explore 探索, journey 旅程, tourist 游客, nomad 游牧者, freedom 自由, abroad 海外, curiosity 好奇心, restlessness 不安分
[记] 不可数。拼写 wander + lust，勿写成 wonderlust。语气偏浪漫、文艺，非临床术语

**sightseeing**
英/美 /ˈsaɪtsiːɪŋ/
n. 观光；游览
[义] The activity of visiting interesting places as a tourist. 作为游客参观名胜的活动（go sightseeing 去观光， sightseeing tour 观光游， sightseeing bus 观光巴士）。不可数。
[例] ① We spent the afternoon sightseeing around the harbor. 我们下午在港口一带观光。 ② The hotel offers a free sightseeing map at reception. 酒店前台提供免费观光地图。
[搭] go sightseeing 去观光, sightseeing tour 观光游, sightseeing bus 观光巴士, sightseeing spot 观光点, do some sightseeing 游览一下
[联] tourist 游客, tour 观光, attraction 景点, landmark 地标, guide 导游, museum 博物馆, monument 纪念碑, camera 相机, map 地图, visit 参观
[记] 不可数。形容词 sightseeing 修饰 tour, bus。see + sights（名胜），固定说 go sightseeing，不说 × go for sightseeing

**destination**
英/美 /ˌdestɪˈneɪʃn/
n. 目的地
[义] The place to which someone or something is going or being sent. 某人或某物将要到达的地方（final destination 最终目的地， tourist destination 旅游目的地， popular destination 热门目的地）。可数。
[例] ① Bali remains a top destination for honeymooners. 巴厘岛仍是蜜月热门目的地。 ② The app shows your destination on the map. 应用会在地图上显示你的目的地。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *destinare*（确定、指向），de- + *stare*（站立）→ 被「定」下来的终点 → 目的地。
[族] destiny 命运, destined 注定的, destine 注定
[搭] final destination 最终目的地, tourist destination 旅游目的地, travel destination 旅行目的地, popular destination 热门目的地, reach one's destination 抵达目的地
[联] departure 出发, arrival 到达, journey 旅程, trip 旅行, airport 机场, hotel 酒店, tourism 旅游业, route 路线, map 地图, visitor 访客
[记] 可数：a destination。destination wedding 目的地婚礼——在异地举办的婚礼

**departure**
英/美 /dɪˈpɑːtʃə(r)/
n. 出发；离开；背离
[义] The act of leaving a place, especially to start a journey; a flight or train that leaves. 离开某地、尤其启程出行；亦指出发的航班或列车（departure lounge 候机厅， departure time 出发时间， departures board 出发显示屏）。可数。
[例] ① Our departure was delayed because of fog. 因大雾，我们的出发延误了。 ② Check the departures board for your gate number. 查看出发显示屏确认登机口。
[源] 源自 depart（离开），de- + *partire*（分开）→ 分开、离去。
[族] depart 离开, department 部门（同 part 根，义转）
[搭] departure lounge 候机厅, departure time 出发时间, departures board 出发屏, point of departure 起点, delayed departure 延误出发
[联] arrival 到达, departure 与 arrival 成对, flight 航班, gate 登机口, airport 机场, schedule 时刻表, delay 延误, journey 旅程, check-in 值机, boarding 登机
[记] 可数：a departure。机场 departures 指出发区；arrivals 指到达区。departure from tradition 指「背离传统」——比喻义

**arrival**
英/美 /əˈraɪvl/
n. 到达；抵达者
[义] The act of reaching a place at the end of a journey; a person or thing that has arrived. 旅程结束时抵达某地；亦指到达的人或物（arrival time 到达时间， new arrival 新到者， arrivals hall 到达大厅）。可数。
[例] ① We waited in the arrivals hall for two hours. 我们在到达大厅等了两个小时。 ② The hotel offers a free shuttle from the airport on arrival. 酒店提供抵达机场后的免费接驳车。
[搭] arrival time 到达时间, arrivals hall 到达大厅, on arrival 抵达时, new arrival 新到者, estimated time of arrival 预计到达时间
[联] departure 出发, flight 航班, airport 机场, hotel 酒店, check-in 入住, luggage 行李, customs 海关, border 边境, welcome 欢迎, schedule 时刻表
[记] 可数：an arrival。ETA = estimated time of arrival。arrival vs. departure 机场 signage 必识

**layover**
英/美 /ˈleɪəʊvə(r)/
n. 中转停留；过夜中转
[义] A period of rest or waiting between parts of a journey, especially a stop between flights. 旅程两段之间的休息或等待，尤指航班之间的经停（long layover 长时间中转， overnight layover 过夜中转）。可数。美式航空语境极常用。
[例] ① We have a six-hour layover in Dubai. 我们在迪拜中转六小时。 ② During the layover, she left the airport to see the city. 中转期间，她出机场看了看城市。
[搭] long layover 长时间中转, overnight layover 过夜中转, layover city 中转城市, during a layover 中转期间, short layover 短中转
[联] stopover 经停, connecting flight 联程航班, transit 过境, airport 机场, flight 航班, delay 延误, lounge 候机厅, departure 出发, gate 登机口, schedule 时刻表
[记] 可数：a layover。layover vs. stopover：常可互换；stopover 有时含「故意中途停留游玩」。英式亦说 stop

**stopover**
英/美 /ˈstɒpəʊvə(r)/
n. 经停；中途停留
[义] A brief stay somewhere in the middle of a long journey, especially when it is intentional. 长途旅行中途的短暂停留，尤指有意为之（stopover in Singapore 在新加坡经停， break the journey with a stopover 中途停留打断行程）。可数。
[例] ① They planned a two-day stopover in Tokyo on the way to Sydney. 他们去悉尼途中计划东京停留两天。 ② The airline allows one free stopover on long-haul tickets. 该航司长途票允许一次免费经停。
[搭] stopover in ……经停, overnight stopover 过夜经停, break the journey with a stopover 中途停留, free stopover 免费经停
[联] layover 中转, transit 过境, connecting flight 联程, destination 目的地, flight 航班, hotel 酒店, journey 旅程, sightseeing 观光, airport 机场, route 路线
[记] 可数：a stopover。动词 stop over 亦可。与 layover 近义，stopover 更强调「中途下来玩一趟」

**transit**
英/美 /ˈtrænzɪt/
n. 过境；中转
v. 经过；运送
[义] The process of moving through a place on the way to somewhere else. 途经某地、前往另一目的地的过程（in transit 运输途中， transit visa 过境签证， transit passenger 中转旅客）。不可数/可数。机场 transit area 指出入境之间的中转区。
[例] ① Your luggage may be checked through if you are only in transit. 若只是中转，行李可能直挂目的地。 ② The package is still in transit and should arrive tomorrow. 包裹仍在运输途中，应明天到。
[搭] in transit 运输途中, transit visa 过境签证, transit passenger 中转旅客, transit lounge 中转候机区, public transit 公共交通（另一常用义）
[联] layover 中转, stopover 经停, connecting flight 联程, airport 机场, visa 签证, passport 护照, customs 海关, passenger 乘客, route 路线, border 边境
[记] 不可数/可数。public transit 指公交系统——旅行章常见另一义。in transit 物流与旅行均用

**itinerary**
英/美 /aɪˈtɪnərəri/
n. 行程；路线
[义] A planned route or journey, or a document listing the places to be visited and times. 计划好的路线或旅程，或列有访问地点与时间的文件（travel itinerary 旅行行程， detailed itinerary 详细行程， change the itinerary 改行程）。可数。
[例] ① The travel agent emailed us a day-by-day itinerary. 旅行社给我们发了逐日行程。 ② Our itinerary includes three cities in one week. 我们一周行程包含三座城市。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *itinerarium*，*iter*（路、旅程）→ 沿路记录 → 行程表。
[族] itinerant 流动的（itinerant worker 流动工人）
[搭] travel itinerary 旅行行程, detailed itinerary 详细行程, day-by-day itinerary 逐日行程, follow the itinerary 按行程走, change the itinerary 更改行程
[联] trip 旅行, tour 观光, schedule 日程, booking 预订, reservation 预约, destination 目的地, flight 航班, hotel 酒店, guide 导游, plan 计划
[记] 可数：an itinerary。复数 itineraries。书面、商务旅行常用；口语可说 schedule 或 plan

**book**
英/美 /bʊk/
v. 预订
n. 书
[义] v. To arrange to have a seat, room, ticket, etc. at a future time. 预订座位、房间、票等（book a flight 订机票， book a hotel 订酒店， book in advance 提前预订）。旅行语境最高频义之一。n. 书——主归属在语言/阅读场，此处仅记预订义。
[形] 过去式 booked；过去分词 booked；现在分词 booking；第三人称 books
[例] ① I booked the hotel online because it was cheaper. 我在网上订了酒店，因为更便宜。 ② The restaurant is fully booked tonight. 餐厅今晚订满了。
[搭] book a flight 订机票, book a hotel 订酒店, book in advance 提前预订, book online 在线预订, fully booked 订满, book a table 订位
[联] booking 预订, reservation 预约, ticket 票, flight 航班, hotel 酒店, room 房间, cancel 取消, confirm 确认, payment 付款, date 日期
[记] 旅行义 book + 机票/酒店/座位。名词 booking 见下条。booked up = fully booked。名词「书」不在此章展开

**booking**
英/美 /ˈbʊkɪŋ/
n. 预订；预约
[义] An arrangement to buy a ticket, stay in a hotel, etc. at a future time. 将来乘机、住店等的预订安排（make a booking 预订， booking confirmation 预订确认， booking reference 预订编号）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Keep your booking confirmation in case the hotel cannot find your reservation. 保留预订确认函，以防酒店找不到预约。 ② Online bookings have to be paid in full upfront. 在线预订须全额预付。
[搭] make a booking 预订, booking confirmation 预订确认, booking reference 预订编号, cancel a booking 取消预订, advance booking 提前预订
[联] book 预订, reservation 预约, hotel 酒店, flight 航班, ticket 票, confirm 确认, cancel 取消, payment 付款, online 在线, receipt 收据
[记] 可数：a booking。booking reference 常简称 reference number。与 reservation 近义，酒店多用 reservation

**reservation**
英/美 /ˌrezəˈveɪʃn/
n. 预订；保留；保留意见
[义] An arrangement for a seat, room, table, etc. to be kept for you. 为你保留座位、房间、餐位等的安排（hotel reservation 酒店预订， table reservation 餐厅订位， make a reservation 预约， reservation number 预订号）。亦指「保留意见」（have reservations about）。可数。
[例] ① I'd like to make a reservation for two nights, starting Friday. 我想预订两晚，从周五开始。 ② The hotel lost our reservation, so they upgraded us. 酒店弄丢了我们的预订，于是给我们升了房。 ③ We made a reservation for eight o'clock under the name Chen. 我们以 Chen 之名预订了餐厅八点的位子。
[源] 源自 reserve（保留），re- + *servare*（保存）→ 为你「留着」→ 预订。
[族] reserve 保留, reserved 矜持的
[搭] hotel reservation 酒店预订, table reservation 餐厅订位, dinner reservation 晚餐预订, make a reservation 预约, reservation number 预订号, confirm a reservation 确认预订, without a reservation 无预订, cancel a reservation 取消预订
[联] booking 预订, hotel 酒店, restaurant 餐厅, room 房间, table 餐桌, confirm 确认, cancel 取消, reception 前台, check-in 入住, availability 空房
[记] 可数：a reservation。have reservations about 对……有顾虑——另一义。餐厅、酒店、航班均可用；英式亦说 booking

**cancellation**
英/美 /ˌkænsəˈleɪʃn/
n. 取消
[义] A decision to stop something that was planned, or the act of doing so; especially of flights, bookings, or tours. 停止原计划的安排，尤指航班、预订或行程（flight cancellation 航班取消， cancellation fee 取消费， free cancellation 免费取消）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The airline offered a refund after the cancellation of our flight. 航班取消后，航司提供了退款。 ② Read the policy on cancellations before you pay. 付款前看清取消政策。
[搭] flight cancellation 航班取消, cancellation fee 取消费, free cancellation 免费取消, last-minute cancellation 临时取消, cancellation policy 取消政策
[联] cancel 取消, booking 预订, reservation 预约, refund 退款, delay 延误, flight 航班, hotel 酒店, policy 政策, confirm 确认, reschedule 改期
[记] 可数/不可数。动词 cancel / canceled, cancelled / canceling, cancelling。英式双写 l

**package tour**
英/美 /ˈpækɪdʒ tʊə(r)/
n. 跟团游；套餐旅游
[义] A holiday organized by a travel company in which travel and accommodation are included in the price. 由旅行社组织、价格含交通与住宿的度假产品（book a package tour 订跟团游， package tour to Spain 西班牙跟团游）。可数。
[例] ① They chose a package tour because they did not want to plan every detail. 他们选了跟团游，因为不想事事自己规划。 ② The package tour includes flights, hotels, and some meals. 套餐含机票、酒店和部分餐食。

[记] 可数：a package tour。英式 package holiday 同义。与 independent travel 自助游对照

**excursion**
英/美 /ɪkˈskɜːʃn/
n. 短途游览；远足
[义] A short journey made for pleasure, often as part of a longer trip. 为休闲而进行的短途出行，常作为较长旅行的一部分（day excursion 一日游， shore excursion 岸上观光——邮轮语境， school excursion 学校郊游）。可数。
[例] ① The cruise offers optional shore excursions at each port. 邮轮在每个港口提供可选岸上观光。 ② We took a bus excursion to the waterfall. 我们乘大巴去瀑布一日游。

[记] 可数：an excursion。比 trip 更正式，常指组织好的短途团。excursion vs. trip：excursion 多「跟团半日一日」

**jet lag**
英/美 /ˈdʒet læɡ/
n. 时差反应
[义] The tired and confused feeling after a long flight across time zones. 长途跨时区飞行后的疲劳与混乱感（ suffer from jet lag 有时差反应， beat jet lag 克服时差， jet lag symptoms 时差症状）。不可数。
[例] ① It took three days to get over the jet lag after the flight to New York. 飞到纽约后，时差反应花了三天才缓过来。 ② She drank water and slept early to reduce jet lag. 她多喝水、早睡以减轻时差。
[搭] suffer from jet lag 有时差, beat jet lag 克服时差, jet lag symptoms 时差症状, recover from jet lag 恢复时差, severe jet lag 严重时差

[记] 不可数。亦写 jetlag。eastward flights 往往比 westward 更难调。无 plural

**airport**
英/美 /ˈeəpɔːt/
n. 机场
[义] A place where aircraft take off and land, with buildings for passengers. 飞机起降并为旅客提供服务的场所（international airport 国际机场， airport terminal 航站楼， airport shuttle 机场接驳）。可数。
[例] ① We arrived at the airport two hours before departure. 我们在出发前两小时到达机场。 ② The new airport terminal opened last month. 新航站楼上月启用。

**terminal**
英/美 /ˈtɜːmɪnl/
n. 航站楼；终点站
adj. 末端的；晚期的
[义] n. A building at an airport where passengers arrive and leave; a station at the end of a transport line. 机场供旅客进出的大楼；交通线路终点站（Terminal 2 二号航站楼， bus terminal 巴士总站）。adj. 末端的——医学 terminal illness 指「晚期」。
[例] ① Our flight leaves from Terminal 3. 我们的航班从三号航站楼出发。 ② Follow the signs to the international terminal. 按指示牌前往国际航站楼。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *terminalis*（边界的），*terminus*（终点）→ 线路终点 → 航站楼。

[记] 可数：a terminal。T1/T2/T3 口语指航站楼编号。形容词 terminal 在医学语境指「晚期」——义别大

**flight**
英/美 /flaɪt/
n. 航班；飞行

[例] ① Our flight was delayed by three hours because of storms. 因风暴，我们的航班延误了三小时。 ② She booked a morning flight to save the afternoon for meetings. 她订了早班机，下午留开会。

**airline**
英/美 /ˈeəlaɪn/
n. 航空公司

[例] ① The airline changed our seats without telling us. 航司没通知就换了我们的座位。 ② Budget airlines often charge extra for checked luggage. 廉航常对托运行李额外收费。

**gate**
英/美 /ɡeɪt/
n. 登机口；大门

[例] ① The flight to Rome is boarding at gate 22. 飞往罗马的航班在 22 号登机口登机。 ② We rushed to the gate after the gate change announcement. 听到更换登机口广播后，我们冲向新登机口。

n. 行李

[记] 不可数。baggage claim 美式；英式常写 baggage reclaim。比喻义 emotional baggage 极常用

[记] 可数：a carry-on。carry on 动词短语指「继续」——同形异义。only carry-on travel 指不托运

**delay**
英/美 /dɪˈleɪ/
n. 延误
v. 推迟；耽搁
[义] n. A situation in which something happens later than planned. 某事晚于计划发生的情况（flight delay 航班延误， without delay 立即， delay of two hours 延误两小时）。v. 使推迟。旅行与交通高频。

**connecting flight**
英/美 /kəˈnektɪŋ flaɪt/
n. 联程航班；中转航班

[例] ① We had only forty minutes between connecting flights. 我们的联程航班之间只有四十分钟。 ② If you miss your connecting flight, the airline may rebook you. 若错过后续航班，航司可能为你改签。

[联] layover 中转, stopover 经停, transit 过境, direct flight 直飞, flight 航班, airport 机场, gate 登机口, delay 延误, luggage 行李, itinerary 行程
[记] 可数：a connecting flight。与 direct/non-stop flight 对照。联程行李可能直挂 final destination

**direct flight**
英/美 /daɪˈrekt flaɪt/
n. 直飞航班
[义] A flight that goes from one place to another without requiring passengers to change planes, though it may stop briefly. 旅客无需换机的航班（可能短暂经停但不换机）。与 non-stop flight（不经停）略有差别，日常常混用。
[例] ① A direct flight to Tokyo takes about twelve hours from here. 从这里直飞东京大约十二小时。 ② Direct flights are usually more expensive than connecting ones. 直飞通常比中转贵。

[记] 可数：a direct flight。direct vs. non-stop：non-stop 强调全程不落地；direct 可能中间停但不换机

**takeoff**
英/美 /ˈteɪkɒf/
n. 起飞
[义] The moment when an aircraft leaves the ground and begins to fly. 飞机离陆开始飞行的时刻（takeoff time 起飞时间， ready for takeoff 准备起飞）。可数/不可数。亦指「（幽默）模仿」——另一义。

[记] 可数/不可数。动词 take off 指起飞；亦指「脱掉」「突然成功」。landing 与 takeoff 成对

**landing**
英/美 /ˈlændɪŋ/
n. 降落；着陆
[义] The act of bringing an aircraft down to the ground. 使飞机降至地面的动作（emergency landing 紧急降落， smooth landing 平稳着陆， landing gear 起落架）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The pilot made a smooth landing despite crosswinds. 尽管有侧风，飞行员仍平稳着陆。 ② Please stow your tray table for landing. 请为降落收起小桌板。

[记] 可数/不可数。动词 land。on landing 抵达时。landing 亦指楼梯平台——另一义

**duty-free**
英/美 /ˌdjuːti ˈfriː/
adj. 免税的
n. 免税商品
[义] Goods that can be bought without paying import tax, especially in airports. 免缴进口税的商品，尤指机场销售（duty-free shop 免税店， duty-free allowance 免税额度）。adj. 修饰 shop, goods。
[例] ① She bought perfume at the duty-free shop before boarding. 登机前她在免税店买了香水。 ② Each passenger has a duty-free allowance for alcohol and tobacco. 每位旅客有烟酒免税额度。
[搭] duty-free shop 免税店, duty-free goods 免税商品, duty-free allowance 免税额度, duty-free price 免税价, buy duty-free 买免税品
[联] airport 机场, customs 海关, shop 商店, perfume 香水, alcohol 酒, tax 税, purchase 购买, terminal 航站楼, price 价格, limit 限额
[记] 形容词 duty-free 常作定语。名词 the duty-free 指免税品区。入境仍可能需申报超额

**lounge**
英/美 /laʊndʒ/
n. 候机厅；休息室
v. 懒洋洋地倚靠
[义] n. A comfortable waiting area in an airport or hotel. 机场或酒店中舒适的等候区（departure lounge 候机厅， business lounge 商务贵宾室， airport lounge 机场休息室）。v. 懒散地靠——另一义。
[例] ① We waited in the departure lounge until boarding began. 我们在候机厅等到开始登机。 ② The credit card gives access to the business lounge. 该信用卡可进商务贵宾室。
[搭] departure lounge 候机厅, business lounge 商务贵宾室, airport lounge 机场休息室, lounge access 贵宾室权益, transit lounge 中转候机区
[联] airport 机场, terminal 航站楼, gate 登机口, waiting 等待, seat 座位, coffee 咖啡, wifi 无线网, boarding 登机, passenger 乘客, comfort 舒适
[记] 可数：a lounge。lounge vs. gate area：lounge 更舒适、常需资格或付费。hotel lounge 指大堂酒吧区

**accommodation**
英/美 /əˌkɒməˈdeɪʃn/
n. 住宿；膳宿
[义] A place to live or stay, especially on holiday or when travelling. 居住或停留之处，尤指度假或旅行中（book accommodation 订住宿， student accommodation 学生宿舍， accommodation costs 住宿费）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① We still need to find accommodation for the last two nights. 最后两晚我们还得找住处。 ② The price includes flights but not accommodation. 价格含机票不含住宿。
[搭] book accommodation 订住宿, accommodation costs 住宿费, student accommodation 学生宿舍, temporary accommodation 临时住宿, accommodation provider 住宿供应方
[联] hotel 酒店, hostel 旅舍, resort 度假村, room 房间, booking 预订, stay 停留, guest 客人, rent 租金, lodging 住宿, travel 旅行
[记] 不可数/可数。英式拼写 accommodation 双 c 双 m。美式 hotel 语境常说 lodging(s)。provide accommodation 提供住宿

**hotel**
英/美 /həʊˈtel/
n. 酒店；旅馆
[义] A building where people pay to stay and eat meals. 供人付费住宿并通常提供餐饮的建筑（boutique hotel 精品酒店， hotel room 客房， hotel reception 酒店前台）。可数。
[例] ① The hotel is a ten-minute walk from the beach. 酒店距海滩步行十分钟。 ② We checked into the hotel after midnight. 我们午夜过后才入住酒店。
[搭] boutique hotel 精品酒店, hotel room 客房, hotel reception 前台, hotel chain 连锁酒店, five-star hotel 五星级酒店, check into a hotel 入住酒店
[联] accommodation 住宿, hostel 旅舍, motel 汽车旅馆, resort 度假村, reception 前台, check-in 入住, reservation 预订, guest 客人, room 房间, luggage 行李
[记] 可数：a hotel。hotel vs. hostel：hotel 服务更全；hostel 更便宜、常共享设施。star rating 星级

**motel**
英/美 /məʊˈtel/
n. 汽车旅馆
[义] A hotel for people who are travelling by car, usually with parking outside each room. 供驾车旅客住宿、通常在房间外设停车位的旅馆（ roadside motel 路边汽车旅馆， motel chain 连锁汽车旅馆）。可数。北美公路旅行典型。
[例] ① We stayed at a cheap motel off the highway for one night. 我们在公路边便宜的汽车旅馆住了一晚。 ② The motel offered free parking in front of each room. 汽车旅馆在每间房前提供免费停车。
[搭] roadside motel 路边汽车旅馆, motel chain 连锁汽车旅馆, budget motel 经济型汽车旅馆, motel room 汽车旅馆房间, stay at a motel 住汽车旅馆
[联] hotel 酒店, highway 公路, parking 停车, road trip 公路旅行, room 房间, drive 驾车, cheap 便宜的, overnight 过夜, accommodation 住宿, rest 休息
[记] 可数：a motel。motor + hotel 合成。多位于公路旁，适合自驾，不一定有餐厅

**hostel**
英/美 /ˈhɒstl/
n. 旅舍；青年旅舍
[义] A cheaper type of hotel where people, especially young travellers, share rooms or facilities. 较便宜的住宿，尤面向年轻旅行者，常共享房间或设施（youth hostel 青年旅舍， hostel dormitory 旅舍多人间， backpacker hostel 背包客旅舍）。可数。
[例] ① We booked a hostel with a shared kitchen to save money. 我们订了带公用厨房的旅舍省钱。 ② The hostel was full of backpackers swapping travel stories. 旅舍里满是交换旅行故事的背包客。
[搭] youth hostel 青年旅舍, hostel dormitory 多人间, backpacker hostel 背包客旅舍, hostel reception 旅舍前台, stay in a hostel 住旅舍
[联] backpacker 背包客, budget 预算, hotel 酒店, dormitory 宿舍, shared 共享的, cheap 便宜的, tourist 游客, accommodation 住宿, kitchen 厨房, bunk bed 双层床
[记] 可数：a hostel。hostel vs. hotel：拼写相近，hostel 更廉价、常共用浴室。hostile（敌意的）勿混

**resort**
英/美 /rɪˈzɔːt/
n. 度假村；度假胜地
v.  resort to 诉诸
[义] n. A place where many people go on holiday, often with hotels and entertainment. 众人度假之地，常含酒店与娱乐（seaside resort 海滨度假胜地， ski resort 滑雪度假村， all-inclusive resort 全包度假村）。可数。v. resort to 指「诉诸」——另一义。
[例] ① The beach resort gets crowded during peak season. 旺季时这家海滨度假村很挤。 ② They stayed at an all-inclusive resort where meals were included. 他们住在全包度假村，餐食包含在内。
[搭] seaside resort 海滨度假胜地, ski resort 滑雪度假村, all-inclusive resort 全包度假村, luxury resort 豪华度假村, resort hotel 度假酒店
[联] hotel 酒店, tourism 旅游业, beach 海滩, vacation 假期, tourist 游客, spa 水疗, pool 泳池, accommodation 住宿, holiday 假期, entertainment 娱乐
[记] 可数：a resort。last resort 最后手段——resort 另一义。peak season 旺季

**inn**
英/美 /ɪn/
n. 客栈；小旅馆
[义] A small hotel or pub, especially in the countryside or in historic areas. 小旅馆或小酒馆，尤在乡村或历史街区（country inn 乡村客栈， coaching inn 驿站）。可数。比 hotel 更小、更有传统或温馨感。
[例] ① We had dinner at a cozy inn near the village. 我们在村旁一家温馨客栈吃了晚饭。 ② The old coaching inn has been welcoming travellers for centuries. 这家老驿站几个世纪以来招待过路旅客。
[搭] country inn 乡村客栈, coaching inn 驿站, family-run inn 家庭经营客栈, stay at an inn 住客栈, historic inn 历史客栈
[联] hotel 酒店, pub 酒馆, village 村庄, countryside 乡村, guest 客人, room 房间, bed 床, breakfast 早餐, traveler 旅人, accommodation 住宿
[记] 可数：an inn。常带历史、乡村、温馨联想。B&B 常比 inn 更小

**check-out**
英/美 /ˈtʃek aʊt/
n. 退房；结账离开
[义] The time when you leave a hotel and pay your bill, or the process of doing so. 离开酒店并结账的时间或过程（check-out time 退房时间， late check-out 延迟退房， express check-out 快速退房）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Check-out is at eleven, but we asked for a late check-out. 退房时间是十一点，但我们申请了延迟退房。 ② She did express check-out through the TV in her room. 她通过房间电视办理了快速退房。
[搭] check-out time 退房时间, late check-out 延迟退房, express check-out 快速退房, at check-out 退房时, check-out desk 退房柜台
[联] check-in 入住, hotel 酒店, reception 前台, bill 账单, room 房间, key 钥匙, luggage 行李, payment 付款, guest 客人, stay 停留
[记] 可数/不可数。动词 check out（无连字符）。supermarket checkout 指收银台——同形异义

**reception**
英/美 /rɪˈsepʃn/
n. 前台；接待
[义] The desk or area in a hotel where guests arrive and staff can help them. 酒店中客人抵达、工作人员提供服务的柜台或区域（hotel reception 酒店前台， reception desk 接待台， reception staff 前台员工）。可数/不可数。亦指「招待会」——另一义。
[例] ① Leave your keys at reception when you go out. 外出时把钥匙留在前台。 ② Reception helped us book a taxi to the airport. 前台帮我们叫了去机场的车。
[搭] hotel reception 酒店前台, reception desk 接待台, reception staff 前台人员, at reception 在前台, 24-hour reception 24 小时前台
[联] check-in 入住, check-out 退房, lobby 大堂, hotel 酒店, guest 客人, key 钥匙, reservation 预订, concierge 礼宾, room 房间, service 服务
[记] 不可数/可数。front desk 美式同 reception。reception vs. concierge：前台办入住；礼宾安排服务

**lobby**
英/美 /ˈlɒbi/
n. 大堂；门厅
v. 游说
[义] n. The area inside the entrance of a hotel or public building. 酒店或公共建筑入口内的大厅（hotel lobby 酒店大堂， wait in the lobby 在大堂等候）。v. 游说——政治义。
[例] ① We met in the hotel lobby before heading to dinner. 去晚餐前我们在酒店大堂见面。 ② Luggage can be stored in the lobby after check-out. 退房后行李可暂存大堂。
[搭] hotel lobby 酒店大堂, lobby bar 大堂吧, wait in the lobby 在大堂等, lobby area 门厅区域, lobby seating 大堂座位
[联] reception 前台, hotel 酒店, entrance 入口, elevator 电梯, guest 客人, sofa 沙发, luggage 行李, check-in 入住, meeting 会面, concierge 礼宾
[记] 可数：a lobby。lobby 政治义指「游说」——旅行语境指门厅。lobby vs. reception：lobby 更大；reception 是柜台

**room**
英/美 /ruːm/
n. 房间；客房
[义] A separate area inside a building with its own walls and door; in hotels, where guests sleep. 建筑物内用墙和门隔开的区域；酒店中供客人睡觉的客房（single room 单人间， double room 双人间， room service 客房服务）。可数。
[例] ① Our room overlooks the garden. 我们的房间对着花园。 ② The hotel upgraded us to a larger room for free. 酒店免费给我们升了更大的房。
[搭] single room 单人间, double room 双人间, hotel room 客房, room service 客房服务, room key 房卡, book a room 订房
[联] hotel 酒店, suite 套房, bed 床, bathroom 浴室, reservation 预订, check-in 入住, guest 客人, view 景观, key 钥匙, clean 清洁
[记] 可数：a room。double room 可能一张大床或两张单人床——预订时须确认。room only 指仅房费不含餐

**suite**
英/美 /swiːt/
n. 套房
[义] A set of rooms in a hotel for one person or family. 酒店中供一人或一家人使用的一组相连房间（executive suite 行政套房， honeymoon suite 蜜月套房， suite with sea view 海景套房）。可数。发音与 sweet 相同。
[例] ① The band stayed in a suite on the top floor. 乐队住在顶楼套房。 ② The honeymoon suite had a balcony overlooking the ocean. 蜜月套房有俯瞰海洋的阳台。
[搭] executive suite 行政套房, honeymoon suite 蜜月套房, suite with sea view 海景套房, penthouse suite 顶层套房, book a suite 订套房
[联] room 房间, hotel 酒店, luxury 豪华, balcony 阳台, bathroom 浴室, living room 客厅, guest 客人, upgrade 升级, view 景观, reservation 预订
[记] 可数：a suite。发音 /swiːt/ 与 sweet 同。suite vs. room：suite 多间相连。software suite 指软件套装——另一义

**vacancy**
英/美 /ˈveɪkənsi/
n. 空房；空缺
[义] A room that is available in a hotel; an unfilled job (another sense). 酒店中可预订的空房间（no vacancy 客满， vacancies available 有空房）。可数。职场义指「职位空缺」——另一常用义。
[例] ① The sign outside said "No Vacancy." 门外招牌写着「客满」。 ② We called three hotels before we found a vacancy. 我们打了三家酒店才找到空房。
[搭] no vacancy 客满, vacancies available 有空房, fill a vacancy 填补空缺, vacancy rate 空房率, check for vacancies 查空房
[联] hotel 酒店, room 房间, booking 预订, reservation 预约, full 满的, available 可用的, sign 招牌, motel 汽车旅馆, stay 停留, occupancy 入住率
[记] 可数：a vacancy。No Vacancy 美式汽车旅馆经典霓虹灯。vacancy 职场义：job vacancy

**room service**
英/美 /ˈruːm sɜːvɪs/
n. 客房服务
[义] A service in a hotel that delivers food and drinks to guests' rooms. 酒店把餐饮送到客房的服务（order room service 叫客房服务， 24-hour room service 24 小时客房服务）。不可数。
[例] ① We ordered room service because we were too tired to go out. 我们太累不想出门，叫了客房服务。 ② Room service is available until midnight. 客房服务供应到午夜。
[搭] order room service 叫客房服务, 24-hour room service 24 小时客房服务, room service menu 客房服务菜单, room service tray 客房餐盘, tip room service 给客房服务小费
[联] hotel 酒店, room 房间, meal 餐, breakfast 早餐, menu 菜单, delivery 配送, guest 客人, tip 小费, order 点餐, restaurant 餐厅
[记] 不可数。动词 order room service。费用常比餐厅贵，并含 service charge

**housekeeping**
英/美 /ˈhaʊskiːpɪŋ/
n. 客房清洁；家务
[义] The department in a hotel that cleans rooms; the work of keeping a house clean. 酒店负责打扫房间的部门；保持居家清洁的工作（call housekeeping 叫客房清洁， housekeeping staff 保洁人员， housekeeping cart 清洁车）。不可数。
[例] ① Housekeeping comes every morning unless you hang the sign. 除非挂免打扰牌，保洁每天上午来。 ② I left a tip for housekeeping on the pillow. 我在枕头上留了给保洁的小费。
[搭] call housekeeping 叫保洁, housekeeping staff 保洁人员, housekeeping cart 清洁车, housekeeping service 清洁服务, do the housekeeping 做家务
[联] hotel 酒店, room 房间, clean 清洁, maid 女服务员, laundry 洗衣, towel 毛巾, bed 床, staff 员工, tip 小费, cart 推车
[记] 不可数。酒店 door hanger：Do not disturb / Please make up room。housekeeper 指保洁员

**concierge**
英/美 /ˈkɒnsieəʒ/
n. 礼宾；门房
[义] A hotel employee whose job is to help guests by booking tickets, restaurants, taxis, etc. 帮客人订票、餐厅、出租车等的酒店员工（ask the concierge 问礼宾， concierge desk 礼宾台， concierge service 礼宾服务）。可数。
[例] ① The concierge booked us a table at a popular restaurant. 礼宾帮我们在热门餐厅订了位。 ② Ask the concierge for directions to the museum. 向礼宾问去博物馆的路。
[源] 源自法语 *concierge*（看门人），经拉丁语 *conservus*（同僚）→ 负责照看宾客的人。
[搭] ask the concierge 问礼宾, concierge desk 礼宾台, concierge service 礼宾服务, hotel concierge 酒店礼宾, concierge recommendation 礼宾推荐
[联] reception 前台, hotel 酒店, guest 客人, reservation 预订, restaurant 餐厅, ticket 票, taxi 出租车, tip 小费, service 服务, recommendation 推荐
[记] 可数：a concierge。发音末尾 /ʒ/ 如 massage。高端酒店礼宾可安排复杂行程

**minibar**
英/美 /ˈmɪnibɑː(r)/
n. 迷你吧
[义] A small fridge in a hotel room with drinks and snacks that guests pay for. 客房内供客人付费取用饮料零食的小冰箱（minibar snacks 迷你吧零食， minibar prices 迷你吧价格， empty the minibar 清空迷你吧）。可数。
[例] ① Minibar prices are listed on the card inside the fridge. 迷你吧价目表在冰箱内的卡片上。 ② He avoided the minibar to save money. 他为省钱没动迷你吧。
[搭] minibar snacks 迷你吧零食, minibar prices 迷你吧价格, stock the minibar 补充迷你吧, minibar charges 迷你吧费用, empty minibar 空迷你吧
[联] hotel 酒店, room 房间, fridge 冰箱, snack 零食, drink 饮料, charge 收费, expensive 贵的, bill 账单, guest 客人, water 水
[记] 可数：a minibar。自动感应扣费，拿起即可能计费——看清政策。自带饮品可不放 fridge

**souvenir**
英/美 /ˌsuːvəˈnɪə(r)/
n. 纪念品
[义] An object you buy or keep to remind you of a place you visited. 购买或保留以纪念某地的物品（souvenir shop 纪念品店， souvenir T-shirt 纪念 T 恤， take home as a souvenir 带回家作纪念）。可数。
[例] ① She bought a small souvenir for each of her colleagues. 她给每位同事买了小纪念品。 ② Cheap souvenirs lined the street near the monument. 纪念碑附近的街上摆满廉价纪念品。
[源] 源自法语 *souvenir*（记忆），拉丁语 *subvenire*（涌上心头）→ 唤起记忆之物 → 纪念品。
[搭] souvenir shop 纪念品店, souvenir T-shirt 纪念 T 恤, take home as a souvenir 作纪念带回, local souvenir 当地纪念品, tacky souvenir 俗气的纪念品
[联] tourist 游客, gift 礼物, shop 商店, memory 记忆, trip 旅行, postcard 明信片, magnet 冰箱贴, landmark 地标, visit 参观, collect 收集
[记] 可数：a souvenir。tacky souvenir 指廉价俗气的旅游品。动词不用 × souvenir the trip

**guidebook**
英/美 /ˈɡaɪdbʊk/
n. 旅行指南
[义] A book that gives information for tourists about a place, its hotels, restaurants, and sights. 为游客介绍某地酒店、餐厅与景点的书（Lonely Planet guidebook  lonely planet 指南， guidebook recommendation 指南推荐， outdated guidebook 过时的指南）。可数。
[例] ① The guidebook listed three affordable hotels near the station. 指南列出了车站附近三家实惠酒店。 ② Guidebooks are useful, but local tips can be better. 指南有用，但当地人建议有时更好。
[搭] travel guidebook 旅行指南, guidebook recommendation 指南推荐, buy a guidebook 买指南, outdated guidebook 过时指南, follow the guidebook 按指南走
[联] guide 导游, tourist 游客, map 地图, itinerary 行程, sightseeing 观光, hotel 酒店, restaurant 餐厅, travel 旅行, tip 贴士, author 作者
[记] 可数：a guidebook。亦说 travel guide。online reviews 常取代纸质指南，但 guidebook 仍常用

**attraction**
英/美 /əˈtrækʃn/
n. 景点；吸引力
[义] A place that people visit for pleasure; something interesting to see. 人们为休闲而参观的地方；值得看的事物（tourist attraction 旅游景点， main attraction 主要看点， free attraction 免费景点）。可数。
[例] ① The old castle is the city's main tourist attraction. 古堡是该城主要旅游景点。 ② The museum offers free entry, so it is a popular attraction. 博物馆免费入场，是热门景点。
[搭] tourist attraction 旅游景点, main attraction 主要看点, free attraction 免费景点, top attraction 顶级景点, local attraction 当地景点
[联] tourist 游客, sightseeing 观光, landmark 地标, museum 博物馆, monument 纪念碑, visit 参观, tour 观光, destination 目的地, crowd 人群, ticket 门票
[记] 可数：an attraction。attraction 亦指「吸引力、爱慕」——语境区分。must-see attraction 必看景点

**landmark**
英/美 /ˈlændmɑːk/
n. 地标；里程碑
[义] A building or feature that is easily recognized and often visited by tourists; an important event. 易于辨认、常为游客参观的建筑或特征；亦指重大事件（historic landmark 历史地标， landmark building 标志建筑， city landmark 城市地标）。可数。
[例] ① The tower has been a city landmark for over a century. 这座塔一个多世纪以来都是城市地标。 ② Our hotel was within walking distance of several landmarks. 我们酒店步行可达多处地标。
[搭] historic landmark 历史地标, landmark building 标志建筑, city landmark 城市地标, famous landmark 著名地标, landmark tour 地标游
[联] monument 纪念碑, attraction 景点, sightseeing 观光, tourist 游客, building 建筑, tower 塔, bridge 桥梁, visit 参观, photo 照片, map 地图
[记] 可数：a landmark。landmark decision 指「里程碑式决定」——比喻义。landmark vs. monument：landmark 更广；monument 常指纪念性构筑物

**cruise**
英/美 /kruːz/
n. 邮轮旅行；巡航
v. 巡航；缓慢行驶
[义] n. A holiday on a large ship that visits several places. 乘大船度假并访问多地的旅行（go on a cruise 乘邮轮， Caribbean cruise 加勒比邮轮， cruise ship 邮轮）。v. 巡航、缓慢开。具名船舶细节主归属交通工具章；此处取度假义。
[例] ① They went on a seven-day cruise around the Mediterranean. 他们参加了环地中海七天邮轮游。 ② The cruise stops at three ports for shore excursions. 邮轮在三个港口停靠供岸上观光。
[搭] go on a cruise 乘邮轮, cruise ship 邮轮, Caribbean cruise 加勒比邮轮, cruise line 邮轮公司, shore excursion 岸上观光
[联] ship 船, excursion 短途游, tourist 游客, port 港口, cabin 舱房, deck 甲板, vacation 假期, tour 观光, sea 海, booking 预订
[记] 可数：a cruise。动词 cruise。river cruise 指内河游轮。seasick 晕船——船上常用

**travel insurance**
英/美 /ˈtrævl ɪnˌʃʊərəns/
n. 旅行保险
[义] Insurance that covers problems such as cancelled trips, medical costs abroad, or lost luggage. 保障行程取消、境外医疗、行李丢失等问题的保险（buy travel insurance 买旅行保险， travel insurance policy 旅行保单， comprehensive travel insurance 综合旅行险）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Always buy travel insurance before an international trip. 国际旅行前务必购买旅行保险。 ② Her travel insurance covered the hospital bill in Thailand. 她的旅行保险覆盖了在泰国的医疗费。
[搭] buy travel insurance 买旅行保险, travel insurance policy 旅行保单, comprehensive travel insurance 综合旅行险, travel insurance claim 旅行保险理赔, medical cover 医疗保障
[联] trip 旅行, flight 航班, cancel 取消, medical 医疗, luggage 行李, policy 保单, claim 理赔, abroad 海外, emergency 紧急, booking 预订
[记] 不可数/可数：a travel insurance policy。申根签证常要求医疗险。read exclusions 看清不保项

**guide**
英/美 /ɡaɪd/
n. 导游；指南
v. 引导
[义] n. A person who shows tourists around a place and explains its history. 带游客参观并讲解历史的人（tour guide 导游， local guide 当地向导， licensed guide 持证导游）。v. 引导。guidebook 见上条。
[例] ① Our tour guide spoke three languages. 我们的导游会说三种语言。 ② The guide led us through the temple and explained the rituals. 导游带我们穿过寺庙并讲解仪式。
[搭] tour guide 导游, local guide 当地向导, licensed guide 持证导游, hire a guide 请导游, guide service 导游服务
[联] tour 观光, tourist 游客, guidebook 指南, sightseeing 观光, group 团队, tip 小费, language 语言, museum 博物馆, explain 讲解, itinerary 行程
[记] 可数：a guide。tip the guide 给小费因国而异。guide vs. guidebook：人 vs. 书

**all-inclusive**
英/美 /ˌɔːl ɪnˈkluːsɪv/
adj. 全包的
[义] Including all meals, drinks, and activities in the price of a holiday. 度假价格中含全部餐食、饮料与活动（all-inclusive resort 全包度假村， all-inclusive package 全包套餐， all-inclusive rate 全包价）。只作形容词。
[例] ① We chose an all-inclusive resort so we would not worry about extra bills. 我们选了全包度假村，免得担心额外账单。 ② Not every drink is included in "all-inclusive" deals—read the fine print. 并非每种饮料都含在「全包」里——看清细则。
[搭] all-inclusive resort 全包度假村, all-inclusive package 全包套餐, all-inclusive rate 全包价, all-inclusive holiday 全包假期, all-inclusive deal 全包优惠
[联] resort 度假村, hotel 酒店, package tour 跟团游, meal 餐, drink 饮料, price 价格, vacation 假期, budget 预算, extra 额外的, buffet 自助餐
[记] 缩写 AI（all-inclusive）。fine print 细则必看——「全包」范围各酒店不同

**security screening**
英/美 /sɪˈkjʊərəti ˌskriːnɪŋ/
n. 安检；安全筛查
[义] The process of checking passengers and luggage for dangerous items before they enter an airport's secure area or board a plane. 旅客与行李进入机场隔离区或登机前的安全检查（go through security screening 过安检， security screening line 安检队伍）。不可数。机场旅行必经环节；security 泛义见冲突与安全章，此处取机场流程义。
[例] ① Allow extra time for security screening during holidays. 节假日请预留额外安检时间。 ② Liquids must be removed from bags at security screening. 安检时须把液体从包里取出。
[搭] go through security screening 过安检, security screening line 安检队伍, security screening checkpoint 安检点, pass security screening 通过安检, security screening rules 安检规定
[联] airport 机场, luggage 行李, carry-on 随身行李, boarding pass 登机牌, gate 登机口, queue 排队, liquid 液体, belt 传送带, scan 扫描, passenger 乘客
[记] 不可数。口语常说 go through security。美国 TSA、英国 security check 同场景区

**baggage claim**
英/美 /ˈbæɡɪdʒ kleɪm/
n. 行李提取处
[义] The area in an airport where arriving passengers collect checked luggage. 机场到达区旅客领取托运行李的区域（proceed to baggage claim 前往行李提取， baggage claim area 行李提取区）。可数/不可数。英式常称 baggage reclaim。
[例] ① We waited at baggage claim for forty minutes after landing. 落地后我们在行李提取处等了四十分钟。 ② Follow the signs to baggage claim and carousel four. 按指示牌前往行李提取处四号转盘。
[搭] proceed to baggage claim 前往行李提取, baggage claim area 行李提取区, baggage claim ticket 行李票, wait at baggage claim 在行李提取处等, lost at baggage claim 提取处丢失
[联] luggage 行李, baggage 行李, carousel 转盘, airport 机场, arrival 到达, suitcase 箱, tag 标签, lost 丢失, claim 领取, flight 航班
[记] 美式 baggage claim；英式 baggage reclaim。claim 在此指「领取」，非「索赔」

**carousel**
英/美 /ˈkærəsel/
n. 行李转盘；旋转木马
[义] A moving belt in an airport where bags are delivered to passengers. 机场传送行李给旅客的旋转带（baggage carousel 行李转盘， carousel number 转盘编号）。可数。亦指游乐旋转木马——另一义。
[例] ① Our bags came out on carousel three. 我们的行李从三号转盘出来。 ② Crowds gathered around the carousel after the international flight. 国际航班到达后，人群围在转盘旁。
[搭] baggage carousel 行李转盘, carousel number 转盘编号, wait at the carousel 在转盘旁等, carousel belt 转盘传送带
[联] baggage claim 行李提取, luggage 行李, suitcase 箱, airport 机场, arrival 到达, wait 等待, belt 传送带, tag 标签, lost 丢失, collect 领取
[记] 可数：carousel 3。baggage carousel 比单说 carousel 更清楚。游乐园 carousel 同词

**shuttle**
英/美 /ˈʃʌtl/
n. 接驳车；穿梭巴士
v. 穿梭往返
[义] A bus or van that travels regularly between two places, such as an airport and a hotel. 在两个地点间定期往返的巴士或面包车（airport shuttle 机场接驳， hotel shuttle 酒店班车， free shuttle 免费班车）。可数。
[例] ① The hotel runs a free shuttle to the airport every hour. 酒店每小时有一班免费机场班车。 ② We missed the last shuttle and took a taxi instead. 我们错过了末班接驳车，改乘出租车。
[搭] airport shuttle 机场接驳, hotel shuttle 酒店班车, free shuttle 免费班车, shuttle bus 穿梭巴士, shuttle service 接驳服务
[联] airport 机场, hotel 酒店, bus 巴士, taxi 出租车, transfer 接送, terminal 航站楼, ride 乘车, schedule 班次, passenger 乘客, driver 司机
[记] 可数：a shuttle。shuttle 亦指航天飞机——另一义。book the shuttle 可提前预约

**lost and found**
英/美 /ˌlɒst ən ˈfaʊnd/
n. 失物招领处
[义] A place in an airport, hotel, or station where items left behind are kept until owners collect them. 机场、酒店或车站保管遗落物品、等候认领的地方（report to lost and found 报失， lost and found office 失物招领处）。不可数/固定短语。
[例] ① She left her scarf on the plane and called lost and found. 她把围巾落在飞机上，打了失物招领电话。 ② The airport lost and found had hundreds of umbrellas. 机场失物招领处堆了几百把伞。
[搭] report to lost and found 报失, lost and found office 失物招领处, contact lost and found 联系失物招领, claim from lost and found 从失物招领领取
[联] luggage 行李, airport 机场, hotel 酒店, lost 丢失, found 找到, item 物品, phone 手机, wallet 钱包, report 报告, claim 领取
[记] 美式 lost and found；英式 lost property (office)。动词不用 × lost-and-founded

**trolley**
英/美 /ˈtrɒli/
n. 手推车；行李车
[义] A small cart with wheels for carrying luggage in an airport or hotel. 机场或酒店用于运送行李的小轮车（luggage trolley 行李车， trolley cart 手推车）。可数。英式常用 trolley；美式亦说 cart。
[例] ① He loaded both suitcases onto a trolley. 他把两个箱子都装上了行李车。 ② Trolleys are usually available near the terminal entrance. 航站楼入口附近通常有行李车。
[搭] luggage trolley 行李车, airport trolley 机场行李车, trolley cart 手推车, push a trolley 推手推车, return the trolley 归还手推车
[联] luggage 行李, suitcase 箱, airport 机场, hotel 酒店, wheel 轮子, cart 手推车, baggage 行李, carry 搬运, queue 排队, deposit 押金
[记] 可数：a trolley。部分机场 trolley 需投币或押金。美式 shopping cart 也叫 cart——语境区分

**overhead bin**
英/美 /ˌəʊvəhed ˈbɪn/
n. 头顶行李舱
[义] The storage compartment above seats in a plane for carry-on luggage. 飞机座位上方存放随身行李的舱位（stow bags in the overhead bin 把包放入头顶舱， overhead bin space 舱内空间）。可数。
[例] ① There was no room left in the overhead bin on our row. 我们那排头顶舱已经没空间了。 ② Please place smaller bags under the seat, not in the overhead bin. 请把小包放座位下，不要占头顶舱。
[搭] overhead bin space 头顶舱空间, stow in the overhead bin 放入头顶舱, full overhead bin 头顶舱已满, overhead bin latch 舱门扣
[联] carry-on 随身行李, seat 座位, flight 航班, boarding 登机, luggage 行李, aisle 过道, pack 打包, space 空间, close 关闭, bag 包
[记] 可数：an overhead bin。亦说 overhead locker（英式）。bin 口语指储物舱

**aisle seat**
英/美 /aɪl siːt/
n. 靠过道座位
[义] A seat next to the aisle on a plane, train, or in a theatre. 飞机、火车或剧院中靠过道的座位（prefer an aisle seat 偏好靠过道， aisle seat request 申请靠过道）。可数。
[例] ① I always book an aisle seat so I can stretch my legs. 我总订靠过道座位，好伸腿。 ② The aisle seat makes it easier to leave during a long flight. 长途飞行靠过道更容易起身离开。
[搭] prefer an aisle seat 偏好靠过道, aisle seat request 申请靠过道, book an aisle seat 订靠过道, aisle or window 过道还是靠窗
[联] window seat 靠窗座, middle seat 中间座, seat 座位, flight 航班, aisle 过道, legroom 腿部空间, boarding 登机, row 排, passenger 乘客, stretch 伸展
[记] 可数：an aisle seat。aisle 发音 /aɪl/，勿读成 /eɪl/。与 window seat 对照选座

**window seat**
英/美 /ˈwɪndəʊ siːt/
n. 靠窗座位
[义] A seat next to a window on a plane or train. 飞机或火车上靠窗的座位（window seat view 窗外风景， choose a window seat 选靠窗）。可数。
[例] ① The child wanted a window seat to watch the clouds. 孩子想要靠窗座位看云。 ② On a clear day, the window seat offers amazing views during landing. 晴天降落时，靠窗座视野极好。
[搭] choose a window seat 选靠窗, window seat view 窗外风景, prefer a window seat 偏好靠窗, window or aisle 靠窗还是过道
[联] aisle seat 靠过道, middle seat 中间座, seat 座位, view 视野, flight 航班, cloud 云, landing 降落, passenger 乘客, photo 拍照, row 排
[记] 可数：a window seat。长途夜航有人偏好靠窗靠壁入睡。middle seat 常最不受欢迎

**boarding group**
英/美 /ˈbɔːdɪŋ ɡruːp/
n. 登机组别
[义] A numbered or labeled category that determines when passengers board a flight. 决定旅客何时登机的编号或分类（boarding group 1 第一组登机， priority boarding group 优先登机组）。可数。
[例] ① Our boarding group was called after families with children. 有孩子的家庭先登机后，才轮到我们组。 ② The airline boards by boarding group to reduce gate crowding. 航司按组别登机以减少登机口拥挤。
[搭] boarding group 1 第一组, priority boarding group 优先组, boarding group number 组别编号, called boarding group 广播某组登机, late boarding group 较晚组别
[联] boarding 登机, gate 登机口, flight 航班, passenger 乘客, priority 优先, queue 排队, announcement 广播, seat 座位, airline 航司, zone 分区
[记] 可数：boarding group 3。各航司分组规则不同；常旅客等级高者组别靠前

**rebook**
英/美 /riːˈbʊk/
v. 重新预订；改签
[义] To book a flight, hotel, or ticket again, usually after a cancellation or change. 在取消或变更后重新预订航班、酒店或票（rebook a flight 改签航班， rebook for free 免费改签， rebook onto another flight 改签到另一班）。及物动词。
[形] 过去式 rebooked；过去分词 rebooked；现在分词 rebooking；第三人称 rebooks
[例] ① The airline rebooked us on the next available flight. 航司把我们改签到最近有空位的航班。 ② Can you rebook the hotel for one extra night? 能把酒店多订一晚吗？
[搭] rebook a flight 改签航班, rebook for free 免费改签, rebook onto another flight 改签到另一班, rebook accommodation 重新订住宿, automatically rebook 自动改签
[联] booking 预订, cancel 取消, delay 延误, flight 航班, reservation 预约, schedule 改期, airline 航司, hotel 酒店, ticket 票, change 变更
[记] re- + book。名词 rebooking。与 reschedule 近义；rebook 更绑「重新订位/订房」

**reschedule**
英/美 /ˌriːˈʃedjuːl/
v. 改期；重新安排时间
[义] To change the planned time of a flight, meeting, or trip. 更改航班、会议或行程的计划时间（reschedule a flight 航班改期， reschedule for tomorrow 改到明天， reschedule at no charge 免费改期）。及物动词。
[形] 过去式 rescheduled；过去分词 rescheduled；现在分词 rescheduling；第三人称 reschedules
[例] ① The storm forced the airline to reschedule dozens of flights. 风暴迫使航司给数十个航班改期。 ② She rescheduled her trip to avoid the holiday rush. 她把行程改期以避开假日高峰。
[搭] reschedule a flight 航班改期, reschedule for tomorrow 改到明天, reschedule at no charge 免费改期, reschedule a meeting 改期会议, ask to reschedule 请求改期
[联] delay 延误, cancel 取消, rebook 改签, booking 预订, flight 航班, date 日期, plan 计划, change 变更, itinerary 行程, appointment 预约
[记] re- + schedule。美式 schedule 发音 /ˈskedʒuːl/。reschedule vs. postpone：reschedule 定新时间；postpone 仅推迟

**overbooking**
英/美 /ˌəʊvəˈbʊkɪŋ/
n. 超售
[义] The practice of selling more seats or rooms than are available, expecting some customers not to show up. 出售的座位或房间数超过实际可用量、赌部分客人不会出现的做法（flight overbooking 航班超售， overbooking compensation 超售补偿， deny boarding due to overbooking 因超售被拒登机）。不可数。
[例] ① Overbooking is common, but passengers may get compensation if bumped. 超售很常见，若被挤掉航班可能获补偿。 ② The hotel apologized for overbooking and moved us to a partner property. 酒店因超售道歉，把我们安排到合作物业。
[搭] flight overbooking 航班超售, overbooking compensation 超售补偿, deny boarding due to overbooking 因超售拒登机, hotel overbooking 酒店超售, policy on overbooking 超售政策
[联] booking 预订, flight 航班, hotel 酒店, seat 座位, cancel 取消, no-show 未到店, compensation 补偿, passenger 乘客, bump 挤掉, volunteer 自愿让位
[记] 不可数。动词 overbook。be bumped 指因超售被改签到下一班。EU 与美国对 compensation 有法规

**upgrade**
英/美 /ʌpˈɡreɪd/
n. 升舱；升级
v. 升级；提升
[义] n. A better seat, room, or service than originally paid for. 比原购更优的座位、房间或服务（free upgrade 免费升舱， upgrade to business class 升到商务舱， room upgrade 房间升级）。v. 升级。旅行与酒店高频。
[例] ① We got a free upgrade to a suite for our anniversary. 结婚纪念日我们免费升到了套房。 ② She used points to upgrade her economy ticket. 她用积分把经济舱票升了级。
[搭] free upgrade 免费升级, upgrade to business class 升商务舱, room upgrade 房间升级, request an upgrade 申请升级, loyalty upgrade 会员升级
[联] seat 座位, room 房间, suite 套房, flight 航班, hotel 酒店, class 舱位, points 积分, loyalty 会员, booking 预订, service 服务
[记] 可数：an upgrade。动词 upgrade / upgraded / upgrading。complimentary upgrade 指免费升级

**passport control**
英/美 /ˈpɑːspɔːt kənˈtrəʊl/
n. 护照检查处
[义] The area at a border or airport where officials check passports and decide whether travelers may enter. 边境或机场官员查验护照、决定是否准许入境的区域（queue at passport control 在护照检查处排队， passport control officer 入境检查官员）。不可数/可数。passport 主条见人生阶段章。
[例] ① Passport control took an hour because of the holiday rush. 假日高峰，护照检查排了一小时。 ② After passport control, we collected our bags at baggage claim. 过护照检查后，我们去行李提取处取包。

[记] 英式常用；美式亦说 immigration or border control。passport 词条见人生阶段章，此处为机场流程名

**airport transfer**

n. 机场接送

[记] 可数/不可数。transfer 在此指「接送服务」，非「转账」。pre-book 可避免到达后排队打车

**key card**
英/美 /ˈkiː kɑːd/
n. 房卡
[义] A plastic card used to open a hotel room door instead of a metal key. 代替金属钥匙开酒店房门的塑料卡（room key card 房卡， swipe the key card 刷卡开门， replace a lost key card 补办房卡）。可数。
[例] ① Reception gave us two key cards for the room. 前台给了我们两张房卡。 ② The key card stopped working, so we went back to reception. 房卡失灵了，我们只好回前台。

**single room**
英/美 /ˈsɪŋɡl ruːm/
n. 单人间
[义] A hotel room intended for one person, usually with one bed. 通常设一张床、供一人使用的客房（book a single room 订单人间， single room supplement 单人间附加费）。可数。
[例] ① Business travelers often book a single room for one night. 出差旅客常订单人间住一晚。 ② The single room was small but had everything I needed. 单人间很小，但所需俱全。

[记] 可数：a single room。single supplement 指跟团或订房单人需加价

**double room**
英/美 /ˈdʌbl ruːm/
n. 双人间
[义] A hotel room for two people, usually with one large double bed. 通常设一张双人床、供两人使用的客房（double room with sea view 海景双人间）。可数。与 twin room 不同：double 常指一大床。

[记] 可数：a double room。预订时确认 double（一大床）还是 twin（两小床）——欧洲酒店尤须问清

**twin room**
英/美 /ˈtwɪn ruːm/
n. 双床间
[义] A hotel room with two separate beds for two people. 有两张分开单人床、供两人使用的客房（twin room with two beds 双床间， twin or double 双床还是大床）。可数。
[例] ① Colleagues on a business trip often share a twin room to save money. 出差同事常合住双床间省钱。 ② I asked for a twin room, not a double. 我要的是双床间，不是大床房。

[记] 可数：a twin room。twin 指两床，不是「双胞胎房间」。英式预订必问 twin vs. double

**bellhop**
英/美 /ˈbelhɒp/
n. 行李员；门童
[义] A hotel employee who carries guests' luggage and shows them to their rooms. 帮客人提行李并引至房间的酒店员工（tip the bellhop 给行李员小费， bellhop service 行李服务）。可数。英式亦说 porter；美式 bellhop 更常见。

n. 客店；招待所

**stay**
英/美 /steɪ/
n. 停留；住宿
v. 停留；暂住
[义] n. A period of living in a place, especially as a guest in a hotel. 在某处生活的时段，尤指酒店作客（ overnight stay 过夜， length of stay 停留天数， enjoy your stay 祝您住得愉快）。v. 停留。旅行住宿核心词。

[记] 可数：a two-night stay。stay vs. live：stay 临时；live 长期定居。hotel sign Enjoy your stay 常见

**room rate**
英/美 /ˈruːm reɪt/
n. 房价

[记] 可数：a room rate。plus tax 美式报价常不含税。BAR = best available rate

**complimentary**
英/美 /ˌkɒmplɪˈmentri/
adj. 免费的；赠送的
[义] Given free as a gift or part of a service, especially in hotels and airlines. 作为礼品或服务一部分免费提供的（complimentary breakfast 免费早餐， complimentary Wi-Fi 免费无线网， complimentary upgrade 免费升级）。只作形容词。比 free 更正式、更绑服务业。

**wake-up call**
英/美 /ˈweɪk ʌp kɔːl/
n. 叫醒电话；警钟
[义] A phone call to a hotel guest at an agreed time to wake them up; also a warning that something serious may happen. 按约定时间打给酒店客人叫醒的电话；亦指严重警告（request a wake-up call 要叫醒服务， wake-up call at six 六点叫醒）。可数。比喻义「警钟」见新闻语境。
[例] ① I requested a wake-up call at five for the early flight. 我为早班机要了五点叫醒。 ② The front desk forgot our wake-up call, and we almost missed checkout. 前台忘了叫醒电话，我们差点误了退房。

[记] 可数：a wake-up call。现代多用手机闹钟，但酒店仍提供此服务。比喻义 a wake-up call 指「警醒」

**no-show**

n. 未到店；未出席

[例] ① The hotel charged a no-show fee because we never arrived. 我们未到店，酒店收了未到店费。 ② Airlines overbook partly because they expect some no-shows. 航司部分因预期会有 no-show 而超售。

**half board**
英/美 /ˌhɑːf ˈbɔːd/
n. 半膳；半包餐
[义] A hotel rate that includes breakfast and one main meal, usually dinner. 含早餐加一顿主餐（通常晚餐）的房价（book half board 订半膳， half board basis 半膳计价）。不可数。与 full board、room only 对照。
[例] ① We booked half board to save time choosing restaurants. 我们订半膳，省得挑餐厅。 ② Half board usually means breakfast and dinner are included. 半膳通常含早晚餐。

[记] 不可数。英式常用；美式更常说 plan with breakfast and dinner。board 在此指「供餐」

**full board**

n. 全膳

[记] 不可数。full board 含三餐；all-inclusive 常还含饮料与活动——范围更广

[例] ① Our cruise cabin had a small window over the sea. 我们的邮轮舱房有一扇小窗对着海。 ② Book an interior cabin if you want the lowest price. 要最低价可订内舱。

[记] 可数：a cabin。飞机 cabin 指客舱整体；cruise cabin 指具体房间。cabin crew 指空乘——职业细节见职业章

**deck**
英/美 /dek/
n. 甲板；层面
[义] One of the floors on a ship; an outdoor floor on a cruise ship where passengers walk. 船上的一层；邮轮供乘客行走的户外甲板（sun deck 阳光甲板， on deck 在甲板上， upper deck 上层甲板）。可数。
[例] ① Passengers gathered on deck to watch the ship leave port. 乘客聚在甲板上看离港。 ② The pool is on the top deck. 泳池在顶层甲板。

[记] 可数：a deck。on deck 口语亦指「准备好了」。飞机 deck 不常用；船/邮轮专用

**seasick**
英/美 /ˈsiːsɪk/
adj. 晕船的
[义] Feeling ill or wanting to vomit because of the movement of a ship or boat. 因船身摇晃而恶心、想吐（feel seasick 晕船， get seasick easily 容易晕船， seasick passenger 晕船乘客）。只作形容词。
[例] ① She felt seasick during the first night on the cruise. 邮轮首夜她晕船了。 ② If you get seasick, sit near the middle of the ship. 若易晕船，坐船中部更稳。

n. 纪念碑；历史遗迹
[义] A building or structure built to remind people of a famous person or event; an important historic site. 为纪念名人或事件而建的构筑物；重要历史遗迹（historic monument 历史遗迹， visit a monument 参观纪念碑， national monument 国家纪念地）。可数。
[例] ① Tourists lined up to see the ancient monument at sunrise. 游客排队在日出时参观古遗迹。 ② The city restored the war monument last year. 该市去年修复了战争纪念碑。

[搭] historic monument 历史遗迹, national monument 国家纪念地, visit a monument 参观纪念碑, ancient monument 古迹, monument site 纪念地
[联] landmark 地标, attraction 景点, tourist 游客, sightseeing 观光, statue 雕像, memorial 纪念馆, visit 参观, heritage 遗产, history 历史, photo 拍照
[记] 可数：a monument。monument vs. landmark：monument 偏纪念性构筑；landmark 更广。 UNESCO 遗产常用 historic monument

**postcard**
英/美 /ˈpəʊstkɑːd/
n. 明信片
[义] A card, often with a picture on one side, that you can send by post without an envelope. 一面常印图、可免信封邮寄的卡片（send a postcard 寄明信片， postcard shop 明信片店， picture postcard 风景明信片）。可数。
[例] ① She sent me a postcard from Venice with a gondola on the front. 她从威尼斯寄来一张正面是贡多拉的明信片。 ② Postcards are still sold near every major monument. 每个大景点旁仍卖明信片。

[记] 可数：a postcard。亦拼 post card。electronic postcards 少见了，实体仍常见于景区

**peak season**
英/美 /ˈpiːk ˈsiːzn/
n. 旺季
[义] The time of year when travel to a place is most popular and prices are highest. 某地旅行最热、价格最高的时段（travel in peak season 旺季出行， peak season rates 旺季价格， avoid peak season 避开旺季）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Peak season rooms must be booked months ahead. 旺季房间须提前数月预订。 ② Everything costs more during peak season. 旺季什么都更贵。

[记] 可数/不可数。peak season vs. high season 常可互换。school holidays 亦会推高 peak season

**off-season**

n. 淡季
adj. 淡季的

[例] ① We saved money by traveling off-season. 淡季出行我们省了钱。 ② Some restaurants close off-season in small beach towns. 小海滨镇有些餐厅淡季关门。

[记] 可数/不可数。亦说 low season。off-season 可能遇天气不佳或设施维护——需权衡

英/美 /ˈkʌstəmz ˌdekləˈreɪʃn/

[例] ① You must declare gifts over the limit on the customs declaration. 超过限额的礼品须在海关申报单上申报。 ② She handed in her customs declaration at the red channel. 她在红色通道交上了申报单。

**duty-free allowance**

n. 免税额度

**entry stamp**
英/美 /ˈentri stæmp/
n. 入境章
[义] A mark placed in your passport when you enter a country, showing the date and place of entry. 入境时在护照上盖的戳记，标明入境日期与地点（passport entry stamp 护照入境章， entry stamp on arrival 抵达入境章）。可数。
[例] ① The officer checked my previous entry stamps before granting a visa extension. 官员在延长签证前查看了我以前的入境章。 ② Some countries no longer use physical entry stamps. 一些国家已不再盖实体入境章。

**business class**
英/美 /ˈbɪznəs klɑːs/
n. 商务舱
[义] A more expensive class of air travel with wider seats, better meals, and more service than economy. 比经济舱更贵、座椅更宽、餐食与服务更好的舱位（business class lounge 商务舱贵宾室， fly business class 坐商务舱）。不可数/可数。

英/美 /spɑː/

[义] A place offering health and beauty treatments with water, steam, or massage, often at a hotel or resort. 提供水疗、蒸汽或按摩等健康美容服务的场所，常见于酒店或度假村（hotel spa 酒店水疗， spa treatment 水疗项目， day spa 日间水疗中心）。可数。
[例] ① The resort spa opens at nine and takes bookings from guests. 度假村水疗九点开门，接待住客预约。 ② After hiking, a spa treatment sounded perfect. 徒步之后，做个水疗再合适不过。

[记] 可数：a self-guided tour。与 guided tour 对照。free vs. paid audio guide 因馆而异

**packing list**

n. 打包清单

**daypack**

[义] A ticket with a flexible return date or no fixed travel date. 返程日期灵活或未固定出行日期的票（open return ticket 开放返程票， valid on open ticket 开放票有效期）。可数。
[例] ① An open ticket let him return within a month when work allowed. 开放票让他在一个月内工作允许时返程。 ② Open tickets are less common on budget airlines now. 廉航现已较少提供开放票。

[记] 可数：an open ticket。火车 open return 英式仍常见；航空较少

**group tour**
英/美 /ˈɡruːp tʊə(r)/
n. 团体游
[义] A tour for a group of people traveling together, often with a guide and fixed itinerary. 一群人一起出行、常有导游与固定行程的游览（join a group tour 参加团体游， small group tour 小团游， group tour guide 团体导游）。可数。

**family room**
英/美 /ˈfæməli ruːm/
n. 家庭房
[义] A hotel room designed for families, often larger with extra beds or space for children. 面向家庭、常更大并含额外床位或儿童空间的客房（book a family room 订家庭房， family room with bunk beds 带 bunk 的家庭房）。可数。

**connecting room**
英/美 /kəˈnektɪŋ ruːm/
n. 连通房
[义] Two hotel rooms with a door between them, booked together for families or groups. 两间客房之间有门相通、常一起预订供家庭或团体使用（book connecting rooms 订连通房， connecting room door 连通门）。可数。常用复数 connecting rooms。
[例] ① Connecting rooms let the parents and kids stay close but separate. 连通房让父母与孩子近而不挤一屋。 ② Connecting rooms are limited, so request them early. 连通房数量有限，须早订。

[记] 可数：connecting rooms。connecting vs. adjoining：connecting 有内门；adjoining 仅相邻

**adjoining room**
英/美 /əˈdʒɔɪnɪŋ ruːm/
n. 相邻房
[义] A hotel room next to another, not necessarily with a connecting door. 与另一客房相邻、未必有连通门的房间（adjoining rooms on the same floor 同层相邻房， quiet adjoining room 安静相邻房）。可数。
[例] ① We asked for adjoining rooms for our friends on the same floor. 我们为朋友要了同层相邻房。 ② Adjoining rooms without a door still share a wall—noise can travel. 无连通门的相邻房仍共墙——噪音会传。

[记] 可数：adjoining rooms。预订时说 adjoining 未必有 connecting door——须明确要求 connecting

**in-room safe**
英/美 /ˌɪn ruːm ˈseɪf/
n. 房内保险箱
[义] A small safe in a hotel room for storing valuables. 酒店客房内存放贵重物品的小保险箱（use the in-room safe 用房内保险箱， in-room safe code 保险箱密码）。可数。
[例] ① Passports and extra cash went into the in-room safe. 护照和备用现金放进房内保险箱。 ② She forgot the in-room safe code and had to call maintenance. 她忘了保险箱密码，只好叫维修。

adj. 售罄的
[义] No longer available because all tickets, rooms, or seats have been sold. 票、房间或座位已全部售出、不再可得（sold out hotel 满房酒店， sold out flight 售罄航班， completely sold out 完全售罄）。只作形容词。

**fully booked**

adj. 订满的
[义] Having no rooms, seats, or places left to book. 无剩余房间、座位或名额可订（fully booked hotel 订满的酒店， fully booked for the weekend 周末订满）。只作形容词。

adj. 未预约的

**e-visa**
英/美 /ˈiː ˌviːzə/
n. 电子签证
[义] A visa applied for and received online before travel, without a sticker in the passport in some systems. 出行前在线申请并获得的签证，部分系统不在护照上贴签（apply for an e-visa 申请电子签， e-visa approval 电子签批准， print e-visa confirmation 打印电子签确认）。可数。visa 主条见人生阶段章。
[例] ① Turkey allows many travelers to use an e-visa at the border. 土耳其允许许多旅客使用电子签入境。 ② Print your e-visa confirmation even if it is linked electronically. 即使电子关联，也打印电子签确认。

**transit visa**

n. 过境签证
[义] A visa that allows you to pass through a country for a short time on the way to another destination. 允许在前往另一目的地途中短期过境一国的签证（need a transit visa 需过境签， 24-hour transit visa 24 小时过境签， transit without visa 免签过境）。可数。visa 主条见人生阶段章。

[联] visa 签证, transit 过境, layover 中转, airport 机场, passport 护照, stopover 经停, border 边境, destination 目的地, hour 小时, zone 隔离区

英/美 /wɜːˈkeɪʃn/
n. 工作度假

[例] ① A workation by the sea worked because Wi-Fi was reliable. 海边工作度假可行，因为无线网稳定。 ② Workations blur the line between office and holiday. 工作度假模糊了办公与假期的界线。

[例] ① Shoulder season offered good weather without summer crowds. 过渡季天气好又没人挤。 ② Hotels lower rates during shoulder season in April and October. 四五十月过渡季酒店降价。

[例] ① Premium economy was worth it on the twelve-hour flight. 十二小时航班坐超级经济舱值得。 ② Premium economy includes a wider seat and better meals. 超级经济舱含更宽座椅与更好餐食。

**codeshare**
英/美 /ˈkəʊdʃeə(r)/
n. 代码共享

[义] A hotel in a historic building preserved for its architectural or cultural value. 位于因建筑或文化价值而受保护的历史建筑中的酒店（stay at a heritage hotel 住历史传承酒店， heritage hotel restoration 历史酒店修复）。可数。
[例] ① The heritage hotel was a converted nineteenth-century bank. 历史酒店由十九世纪银行改建。 ② Heritage hotels often have creaky floors but rich character. 历史酒店常地板吱呀，但韵味十足。

[联] boutique hotel 精品酒店, historic 历史的, building 建筑, restoration 修复, character 特色, room 房间, stay 住宿, architecture 建筑, hotel 酒店, culture 文化
[记] 可数：a heritage hotel。可能无 elevator 或 modern AC——订前查设施

**micro-stay**
英/美 /ˈmaɪkrəʊ steɪ/
n. 微停留
[义] A very short hotel booking, often by the hour, for rest between flights. 极短酒店预订，常按小时计，供航班间休息（book a micro-stay 订微停留， airport micro-stay 机场微停留）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① A micro-stay of four hours let us shower between connections. 四小时微停留让我们在联程间洗了澡。 ② Micro-stay apps partner with airport hotels. 微停留应用与机场酒店合作。

[记] 不可数/可数。与 day room 近义——micro-stay 更强调按小时计费平台

[例] ① A luggage scale at home saved us excess baggage fees. 在家用行李秤省了超重费。 ② The luggage scale showed we were two kilos over the limit. 行李秤显示我们超重两公斤。

英/美 /sliːp mɑːsk/
n. 睡眠眼罩
[义] A mask worn over the eyes to block light while sleeping on a trip. 旅行睡觉时遮光用的眼罩（pack a sleep mask 带睡眠眼罩， airline sleep mask 航班睡眠眼罩）。可数。
[例] ① A sleep mask helped her nap on the red-eye flight. 睡眠眼罩帮她在红眼航班上打盹。 ② The hotel left a sleep mask with the turn-down service. 酒店夜床服务放了睡眠眼罩。

**port day**
英/美 /pɔːt deɪ/
n. 停靠日
[义] A day on a cruise when the ship is in port and passengers can go ashore. 邮轮停靠港口、乘客可上岸的那一天（port day excursion 停靠日观光， sea day vs. port day 海上日与停靠日）。可数。
[例] ① Port days were packed with shore excursions. 停靠日排满了岸上观光。 ② On port day the ship arrived at seven and left at six. 停靠日船七点抵、六点离。

**sea day**
英/美 /siː deɪ/
n. 海上日
[义] A day on a cruise when the ship is at sea with no port stop. 邮轮在海上航行、不靠港的一天（sea day activities 海上日活动, sea day schedule 海上日安排, port day vs. sea day 停靠日与海上日）。可数。
[例] ① Sea days were for the pool, shows, and long lunches. 海上日用来游泳、看秀、慢午餐。 ② On our first sea day the ship crossed open ocean. 第一个海上日船穿越开阔洋面。

**Schengen visa**
英 /ˈʃeŋən ˈviːzə/ 美 /ˈʃeŋən ˈviːzə/
n. 申根签证
[义] A visa that allows travel within most European countries in the Schengen Area. 允许在申根区大多数欧洲国家旅行的签证（apply for a Schengen visa 申请申根签证, Schengen visa stamp 申根签证章, 90-day Schengen visa 九十日申根签）。可数。
[例] ① She applied for a Schengen visa at the embassy in Beijing. 她在北京使馆申请申根签证。 ② The Schengen visa allowed travel across twenty-six countries. 申根签证可通行二十六国。
[搭] apply for a Schengen visa 申请申根签证, Schengen visa stamp 申根签证章, 90-day Schengen visa 九十日申根签, Schengen visa application 申根签申请
[联] visa 签证, passport 护照, embassy 大使馆, border 边境, Europe 欧洲, travel 旅行
[记] 可数：a Schengen visa。通常 90/180 天规则——停留须合规

**ESTA**
英 /ˈestə/ 美 /ˈestə/
n. 美国旅行授权
[义] Electronic System for Travel Authorization: online approval for visa-free travel to the US from eligible countries. 旅行授权电子系统：符合条件的国家旅客免签赴美前的在线批准（apply for ESTA 申请 ESTA, ESTA approval ESTA 批准, valid ESTA 有效 ESTA）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① ESTA approval arrived within minutes of the online form. 在线表格提交后几分钟内 ESTA 即批。 ② Without a valid ESTA, check-in staff cannot issue a boarding pass. 无有效 ESTA，值机员无法发登机牌。
[搭] apply for ESTA 申请 ESTA, ESTA approval ESTA 批准, valid ESTA 有效 ESTA, ESTA authorization 旅行授权
[联] visa 签证, passport 护照, United States 美国, travel 旅行, authorization 授权, check-in 值机
[记] 通常读 /ˈestə/。与签证不同——授权而非贴纸；有效期两年

**Global Entry**
英/美 /ˈɡləʊbl ˈentri/
n. 全球通关计划

[例] ① Global Entry cut our airport exit time to under ten minutes. 全球通关把出机场时间缩到十分钟内。 ② She used the Global Entry kiosk instead of the long queue. 她走全球通关自助机而非长队。

[联] passport control 护照检查, customs 海关, immigration 入境, airport 机场, TSA PreCheck 预检, queue 排队

**baggage allowance**
英/美 /ˈbæɡɪdʒ əˈlaʊəns/

[例] ① Economy baggage allowance was one checked bag of twenty-three kilos. 经济舱额度为一件二十三公斤托运行李。 ② Business class doubled the baggage allowance. 商务舱行李额度翻倍。

[联] luggage 行李, suitcase 行李箱, excess baggage 超重行李, airline 航司, fee 费用, weight 重量
[记] 不可数/可数。low-cost 航司常另收费——订前查 fare rules

**minimum connection time**
英/美 /ˈmɪnɪməm kəˈnekʃn taɪm/
n. 最短转机时间
[义] The shortest legal interval between two flights on one booking at an airport. 同一预订在同一机场两班机之间允许的最短间隔（ meet minimum connection time 满足最短转机时间, tight minimum connection time 紧张的最短转机, MCT 缩写）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The booking violated minimum connection time at Heathrow. 该预订违反希思罗最短转机时间。 ② Airlines will not sell itineraries below minimum connection time. 航司不会出售低于最短转机时间的行程。

**overwater bungalow**
英/美 /ˈəʊvəwɔːtə ˌbʌŋɡəˈluː/

英/美 /ˈbʊfeɪ/ 美 /bəˈfeɪ/
n. 自助餐
[义] A meal where guests serve themselves from a table of many dishes, common in hotels. 客人从摆满菜品的餐桌自行取餐的用餐方式，酒店常见（breakfast buffet 自助早餐, all-you-can-eat buffet 无限量自助, hotel buffet 酒店自助餐）。可数。
[例] ① The breakfast buffet included fresh fruit and local pastries. 自助早餐有新鲜水果和本地糕点。 ② We skipped lunch and went straight to the dinner buffet. 我们跳过午餐，直接吃晚餐自助。
[搭] breakfast buffet 自助早餐, all-you-can-eat buffet 无限量自助, hotel buffet 酒店自助餐, buffet breakfast 自助早, seafood buffet 海鲜自助
[联] hotel 酒店, breakfast 早餐, all-inclusive 全包, restaurant 餐厅, serve 上菜, plate 盘子, variety 多样, guest 客人, meal 一餐, dessert 甜点
[记] 可数：a buffet。发音英式 /ˈbʊfeɪ/，美式常 /bəˈfeɪ/。buffet 动词 /ˈbʌfɪt/ 指「猛击/冲击」，不同义

**safe**
英/美 /seɪf/
n. 保险箱
adj. 安全的
[义] n. A strong metal box in a hotel room or bank for storing valuables. 酒店房间或银行内存放贵重物品的坚固金属箱（hotel safe 酒店保险箱, in-room safe 房内保险箱, put valuables in the safe 把贵重物品放保险箱）。adj. 安全。
[例] ① She locked her passport in the in-room safe. 她把护照锁进房内保险箱。 ② Is it safe to leave my laptop in the hotel room? 笔记本电脑放酒店房间安全吗？
[搭] hotel safe 酒店保险箱, in-room safe 房内保险箱, put valuables in the safe 贵重物品放保险箱, electronic safe 电子保险箱, safe deposit box 保管箱
[联] hotel 酒店, room 房间, passport 护照, valuables 贵重物品, lock 锁, key 钥匙, secure 安全的, money 钱, jewellery 珠宝, reception 前台
[记] 可数：a safe。safe deposit box 在银行；hotel 多用 in-room safe。形容词 safe 极高频

**consulate**
英/美 /ˈkɒnsjələt/
n. 领事馆
[义] The office of a foreign country that handles visas and help for its citizens abroad. 外国政府办理签证与海外公民事务的机构（local consulate 当地领事馆, consulate appointment 领馆预约, visit the consulate 赴领馆）。可数。
[例] ① She booked a consulate appointment for her visa interview. 她预约了领馆签证面试。 ② The consulate issued emergency travel documents. 领事馆签发了紧急旅行证件。
[搭] local consulate 当地领事馆, consulate appointment 领馆预约, visit the consulate 赴领馆, consulate visa section 签证处
[联] embassy 大使馆, visa 签证, passport 护照, appointment 预约, interview 面试, travel document 旅行证件
[记] 可数：a consulate。embassy 多在大首都；consulate 在主要城市

**embassy**
英/美 /ˈembəsi/
n. 大使馆
[义] The main diplomatic office of one country in another's capital. 一国在另一国首都的主要外交机构（embassy visa section 使馆签证处, contact the embassy 联系大使馆, embassy security 使馆安保）。可数。
[例] ① Visa applications for long stays go through the embassy. 长期停留签证申请走大使馆。 ② The embassy advised citizens to leave the region. 使馆建议公民离开该地区。
[搭] embassy visa section 使馆签证处, contact the embassy 联系大使馆, embassy security 使馆安保, go to the embassy 前往大使馆
[联] consulate 领事馆, visa 签证, diplomat 外交官, passport 护照, country 国家, security 安全
[记] 可数：an embassy。紧急领事协助可联系 nearest embassy/consulate

**in-flight meal**
英/美 /ɪn flaɪt miːl/
n. 机上餐
[义] Food served to passengers during a flight. 飞行期间提供给乘客的餐食（in-flight meal service 机上供餐, special in-flight meal 特殊餐, pre-order in-flight meal 预订机上餐）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The in-flight meal on the long haul was surprisingly good. 长途航班机上餐出乎意料地好。 ② Vegetarian in-flight meals must be ordered in advance. 素食机上餐须提前预订。
[搭] in-flight meal service 机上供餐, special in-flight meal 特殊餐, pre-order in-flight meal 预订机上餐, in-flight meal choice 餐食选择
[联] flight 航班, airline 航司, meal 餐食, vegetarian 素食的, service 服务, long-haul 长途
[记] 不可数/可数。short-haul 可能仅 snack；special meal 代码如 VGML

**tray table**
英/美 /treɪ ˈteɪbl/
n. 小桌板
[义] The folding table attached to the seat in front of you on a plane. 飞机前排座椅背后可折叠的小桌（fold down the tray table 放下小桌板, stow tray table 收起小桌板, broken tray table 损坏小桌板）。可数。
[例] ① Stow your tray table for landing. 着陆请收起小桌板。 ② The tray table wobbled every time the person in front moved. 前排一动小桌板就晃。
[搭] fold down the tray table 放下小桌板, stow tray table 收起小桌板, broken tray table 损坏小桌板, tray table latch 桌板锁扣

[记] 可数：a tray table。landing/turbulence 前须收起

**compression socks**
英/美 /kəmˈpreʃn sɒks/
n. 压缩袜
[义] Tight socks worn on long flights to improve circulation and reduce swelling. 长途飞行穿着、改善循环、减轻肿胀的紧袜（wear compression socks 穿压缩袜, flight compression socks 飞行压缩袜, pack compression socks 带压缩袜）。复数。
[例] ① Compression socks helped her ankles on the twelve-hour flight. 十二小时航班上压缩袜缓解了脚踝肿胀。 ② Doctors often recommend compression socks for long-haul travel. 医生常建议长途旅行穿压缩袜。
[搭] wear compression socks 穿压缩袜, flight compression socks 飞行压缩袜, pack compression socks 带压缩袜, knee-high compression socks 及膝压缩袜
[联] long-haul 长途, flight 航班, swelling 肿胀, circulation 循环, travel 旅行, health 健康
[记] 复数。亦说 compression stockings；DVT 预防相关建议常见

**high season**
英/美 /haɪ ˈsiːzn/
n. 旺季
[义] The time of year when travel demand and prices are highest. 旅行需求与价格最高的时节（high season rates 旺季价格, travel in high season 旺季出行, peak vs. high season 高峰与旺季）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① High season rates doubled the cost of the beach hotel. 旺季价格使海滩酒店费用翻倍。 ② Book early if you travel in high season. 旺季出行请早订。
[搭] high season rates 旺季价格, travel in high season 旺季出行, high season booking 旺季预订, avoid high season 避开旺季
[联] peak season 旺季, off-season 淡季, shoulder season 肩季, price 价格, booking 预订, holiday 假期
[记] 不可数/可数。与 peak season 近义；off-season / shoulder season 相对

**blackout date**
英/美 /ˈblækaʊt deɪt/
n. 不可用日期
[义] A date when a discount, miles redemption, or promotion cannot be used. 折扣、里程兑换或促销不可用的日期（holiday blackout dates 假日 blackout, no blackout dates 无 blackout, blackout date restriction blackout 限制）。可数。常用复数 blackout dates。
[例] ① Blackout dates blocked our points redemption over Christmas. 圣诞节期间 blackout 日期使积分兑换不可用。 ② The promotion had blackout dates during school holidays. 促销在学校假期有 blackout 日期。

**last-minute booking**
英/美 /ˌlɑːst ˈmɪnɪt ˈbʊkɪŋ/
n. 临时预订
[义] A reservation made very close to the date of travel or stay. 临近出行或入住日才下的预订（last-minute booking deal 临时预订优惠, make a last-minute booking 临时预订, last-minute hotel booking 临时订酒店）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① A last-minute booking saved thirty percent on the hotel. 临时预订省了酒店三成。 ② Last-minute bookings risk sold-out flights. 临时订机票可能售罄。

[记] 不可数/可数。可能捡漏也可能更贵——视供需

**seat pitch**
英/美 /siːt pɪtʃ/
n. 座椅间距
[义] The space between a point on one seat and the same point on the seat in front, affecting legroom. 同一座椅点与前排同一点之间的距离，影响腿部空间（seat pitch in economy 经济舱间距, extra seat pitch 额外间距, narrow seat pitch 窄间距）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The airline advertised generous seat pitch in premium economy. 航司宣传 premium economy 座椅间距宽裕。 ② Narrow seat pitch made the six-hour flight uncomfortable. 窄间距使六小时航班不舒服。

[记] 不可数/可数。pitch 越大腿越舒服；与 seat width 不同

**liquids rule**
英/美 /ˈlɪkwɪdz ruːl/
n. 液体携带规定
[义] Airport security limits on liquids in carry-on bags, typically 100 ml containers in one clear bag. 安检对随身液体限制，通常要求百毫升容器装于透明袋（liquids rule 100 ml 百毫升液体规定, comply with liquids rule 遵守液体规定, liquids rule bag 液体规定袋）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The liquids rule forced her to discard a full shampoo bottle. 液体规定迫使她丢弃整瓶洗发水。 ② Each container must be under 100 ml under the liquids rule. 液体规定要求每容器低于一百毫升。

英/美 /ˈsiːtbæk skriːn/
n. 椅背屏幕
[义] A personal entertainment screen in the seatback on a plane. 飞机椅背上的个人娱乐屏幕（seatback screen entertainment 椅背娱乐, broken seatback screen 损坏椅背屏, touch-screen seatback 触控椅背屏）。可数。

n. 护照照片

**biometric passport**
英/美 /ˌbaɪəʊˈmetrɪk ˈpɑːspɔːt/
n. 生物识别护照

[例] ① The gate scanned her biometric passport in seconds. 闸门几秒内扫描了她的生物识别护照。 ② Biometric passports are required for ESTA travel to the US. 赴美 ESTA 旅行需生物识别护照。

n.  pension；小旅馆
[义] A small, often family-run guesthouse in Europe, especially German-speaking regions. 欧洲、尤德语区常见的小型家庭旅馆（stay at a pension 住 pension, family pension 家庭 pension, mountain pension 山区 pension）。可数。与「养老金」pension 同形，语境区分。

**waitlist**

n. 候补名单

[义] A list of people waiting for seats or rooms if others cancel. 他人取消时等候座位或房间的名单（join the waitlist 加入候补, waitlist clearance 候补成功, hotel waitlist 酒店候补）。可数/动词。

**city tax**

n. 城市税
[义] A local tax added to hotel bills in many European cities. 许多欧洲城市加在酒店账单上的地方税（pay city tax 付城市税, city tax per night 每晚城市税, city tax at check-in 入住时收城市税）。可数/不可数。

**flash sale**
英/美 /flæʃ seɪl/
n. 闪购
